SALMON RIVER AT CHASM FALLS NY, USGS site 04270000 (USGS-04270000), stream in Franklin County, New York, draining 132 square miles. Daily means from 1925-07-24 to 2013-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
